AI疑慮未除，科技股震盪加劇
2026年 11月 25日
一、市場表現
1. 美股走勢分歧，科技股反彈未能延續：美股 11月 24日走勢分歧，前一交易日因黃仁
勳重返輝達所帶動的科技股強勁反彈未能延續，AI 及半導體類股再度面臨沉重賣壓，
費城半導體指數重挫 7.9%，那斯達克綜合指數下跌 1.48%。相較之下，非科技類股表
現相對穩健，道瓊工業指數逆勢上漲，顯示資金仍存在產業輪動現象，而非全面撤離股
票市場。
表一：美股主要指數 11/24表現
指數 收盤 漲跌幅
道瓊工業指數 50,018.70 +0.85%

標普 500指數 7,201.09 −0.50%
那斯達克綜合指數 23,733.54 −1.48%
費城半導體指數 8,832.50 −7.88%
資料來源：Bloomberg、元大銀行整理
2. AI相關個股跌幅居前，防禦型類股相對抗跌：個股方面，輝達（NVDA）繼 11月 23
日大漲 31.5%後，11月 24日回跌 5.9%；AI雲端運算服務商 CoreWeave重挫 11.8%，
電力設備商 GE Vernova下跌 4.8%，甫上市的 AI模型開發商 Anthropic亦下跌 6.5%。
相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費等防禦型類股表現較佳，反映市場賣壓仍主要集中於
AI相關投資領域。
二、下跌原因分析
1. 黃仁勳回歸僅解除部分經營疑慮，科技股反彈未能延續：輝達 11月 23日宣布黃仁勳
重返執行長職位，一度大幅提振市場信心，當日輝達股價飆漲 31.5%，標普 500及那
斯達克指數分別大漲約 4.7%及 6.5%。然而，美國兩黨國會議員對黃仁勳回歸仍有批評
聲浪，並主張進一步限制中國取得輝達先進 AI晶片。黃仁勳回歸雖有助於維持輝達產

品開發與長期經營策略的延續性，但公司治理、國家安全及晶片出口管制等問題尚未完
全落幕，前一交易日因事件利多所帶動的買盤缺乏持續性。
2. 美國可能對 16個貿易夥伴加徵關稅，政策不確定性再起：據 Reuters 11/24報導，川
普政府可能針對 16個貿易夥伴推出新一輪關稅措施，涉及中國及歐盟等主要經濟體，
相關消息使市場重新評估全球貿易環境及通膨風險。由於半導體產業高度仰賴跨國供應
鏈，若關稅措施進一步擴大，恐增加伺服器、晶片及其他電子零組件的生產成本，並影
響企業資本支出規劃。此外，關稅亦可能增加聯準會抑制通膨的難度，對科技股評價形
成額外壓力。惟相關措施尚未正式公布，實際影響仍須視課徵範圍及稅率而定。

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI投資報酬疑慮與升息壓力未解，高評價族群首當其衝：市場對 AI產業的疑慮，主要
集中於龐大資本支出能否創造相應的投資報酬，加上市場對資料中心監管的疑慮，AI
基礎建設的擴張速度仍存在不確定性。另一方面，市場對聯準會 12月升息已有高度預
期，且關注 2027年是否仍將持續升息，在 11月 25日 PCE物價指數公布前，高評價
及高資本支出的 AI相關企業仍承受評價壓力，賣壓集中於 AI雲端服務、電力設備及新
上市 AI企業等族群。
4. 多重變數未除，短線波動偏高：除了輝達本身的議題，美股後續走勢仍面臨兩大變數，

一是聯準會的升息步調，二是 AI資料中心的興建進度可能受阻。在升息方面，如果聯
準會 12月的升息幅度或未來的升息路徑超出市場預期，科技股評價恐再度受到壓抑。
在資料中心方面，美國部分地方政府陸續限制資料中心興建，可能延後雲端大廠 AI投
資計畫的落實時程，市場對 AI投資前景的疑慮短期內恐難消除。在這兩項變數明朗之
前，美股短期波動預料仍偏高，科技股各次產業的表現也可能進一步分化。因此，建議
投資人分批佈局，避免在市場反彈時過度追價，並保留部分資金，以因應後續市場波動。
圖一:近期主要市場風險
三、市場看法
1. 費半劇烈震盪反映市場信心脆弱，惟並非全然回吐前日漲幅：費城半導體指數 11月 20
日重挫 10.73%，11月 23日旋即大漲 14.03%，11月 24日再度重挫約 7%至 8%，顯
示市場仍處於高度不穩定的階段。然而，若以 Reuters所報導的 11月 24日約 7.9%跌
幅計算，費半指數近兩個交易日累計仍上漲約 5.0%；輝達同期累計漲幅亦約達 23.7%，
顯示黃仁勳回歸所帶來的正面影響並未完全消失。我們認為，11月 24日的下跌部分反

映前一交易日漲幅過大後的獲利了結，以及投資人重新評估尚未解除的風險，而非全然
出現新的產業基本面利空。
2. 輝達治理與政策風險仍是科技股最大變數，短線股價恐持續劇烈波動：黃仁勳回任後首
場全員工大會為違反公司行為準則致歉，並表示輝達不需要傳統接班計畫，反而加深外
界對公司治理的疑慮。此外，美國參議院將於 12月 3日就晶片出口管制舉行聽證會，
黃仁勳將出席；參議院民主黨領袖舒默亦表示將調查政府及輝達高層在復職決定中的角
色，加上路透報導 SEC 正調查其會計作業，相關議題短期內恐持續牽動股價。輝達基

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
本面仍受 AI算力需求支撐，但在治理、出口管制及監管調查明朗前，其股價及對費半
與台灣供應鏈的影響恐維持高波動，投資人宜避免過度集中單一個股。
3. AI算力需求尚未反轉，惟資料中心建設遞延恐影響營收認列時程：輝達 11月 17日公
布的財報仍為 AI需求提供正面佐證：第三季營收達 1,158.4億美元，年成長逾一倍，
優於市場預期的 1,091.1 億美元；第四季營收財測 1,300 億美元（上下 3%），亦高於
預期的 1,252.5億美元，且未納入中國資料中心晶片銷售。股價下跌主要反映市場對需
求成長速度及可持續性的疑慮，而非營運已明顯衰退。另一方面，資料中心監管疑慮主

要影響 AI基礎建設擴張速度，而非長期導入需求，企業仍可透過轉移建設地點、提升
運算效率或租用其他雲端算力因應，惟建設遞延仍可能影響半導體企業出貨及營收認列
時程。我們對 AI產業長期發展仍持正向看法，但不宜低估未來數季財測下修風險，須
關注主要 CSP是否削減資本支出、取消訂單或遞延設備採購。
4. 科技股評價修正有助改善中期風險報酬，投資邏輯轉向個別企業獲利品質的篩選：根據
Reuters 11月 9日報導，標普 500科技類股本益比已一度低於整體標普 500指數，評
價溢價明顯收斂。若企業獲利預估未同步大幅下修，股價修正將有助改善中長期風險報
酬，惟評價降低不代表已經落底，市場仍面臨公司治理、政策、關稅、利率及 AI投資
回收等多重不確定因素。此外，AI 產業鏈各環節的獲利能力及資金需求差異明顯，具
備技術門檻、定價能力及穩健現金流的企業，與高度仰賴外部融資、獲利模式未成熟者，
股價表現將更趨分化。後續需觀察獲利預估是否止穩、股價對利空反應是否鈍化，以及
反彈能否延續，才能確認信心修復。

5. Anthropic上市後表現疲弱，反映市場對 AI商業化的要求提高：Anthropic於 11月
19日掛牌後，股價未能延續初期表現，11月 24日再跌 6.5%。AI模型開發商為算力
需求的重要來源，若資本市場對其評價及融資轉趨審慎，長期恐影響算力擴張速度。不
過，股價疲弱亦可能反映 IPO 定價偏高、獲利不確定性及風險偏好降低，不能直接推
論 AI終端需求下降。後續應更重視 AI企業的營收成長、客戶採用程度、推論成本及現
金消耗速度；若模型使用量持續增加且單位運算成本下降，仍有利長期算力需求。
6. 美股產業輪動持續，科技股重挫尚未演變為全面性基本面惡化：11月 24日費半大跌，
道瓊卻逆勢上漲 0.85%，兩者相差逾 8 個百分點，反映資金仍留在股市，只是由高評
價的 AI類股轉向其他產業。根據 Associated Press報導，標普 500企業有望連續第三
季繳出逾 25%的獲利成長，美國消費者信心亦升至近兩年高點，尚不宜將科技股修正
定調為經濟衰退或多頭格局終結。若非科技產業獲利穩健，資金輪動反有助降低美股對

少數大型科技股的依賴。
7. 12月 FOMC升息與否並非唯一關鍵，後續政策路徑更為重要：由於市場對 12月升息
已有高度預期，若聯準會如預期升息 1碼，其對股市的額外衝擊可能相對有限。更重要
的是，聯準會是否透過會後聲明、最新經濟預測及主席記者會，暗示 2027年仍將持續
升息。若 12月升息後，聯準會轉而強調觀察累積緊縮效果，科技股評價壓力可望逐步
緩和；反之，若通膨預測上修，且官員暗示明年仍有多次升息的必要，則高評價及高資
本支出企業仍可能承壓。因此，11月 25日公布的 PCE物價指數及 12月 FOMC的政
策指引，將是研判科技股後續走勢的重要觀察指標。

不另行通知。
四、市場展望與投資建議
1. 短線審慎，中期審慎偏正向，關注企業獲利與資金面變化：本波全球科技股修正已由
初期的評價調整，進一步擴大至企業治理、政策風險及資金面的全面性重新定價。尤
其費半指數單日重挫超過 10%，顯示市場風險偏好快速下降，短期賣壓尚未完全解除
。然而，目前尚無充分證據顯示 AI運算需求已出現全面性反轉，輝達最新財報及主要
CSP持續投入 AI基礎建設，仍可為產業中長期成長提供一定支撐。因此，我們對美國
科技股及具備 AI供應鏈優勢的台灣、日本市場，短期維持審慎，中期則採取審慎偏正
向的觀點。不過，科技股未來表現將更依賴實際獲利、訂單能見度及自由現金流，而

非單純的 AI題材。後續應持續關注輝達 SEC調查、CSP資本支出、Fed政策及信用
風險是否擴散；若相關疑慮逐步釐清、企業獲利預估保持穩定，股市可望逐漸回歸基
本面主導。策略上，對中長期仍看好科技產業的投資人，可透過以分批布局及分散類
股配置方式參與，在科技類股部分，應優先考量具備技術競爭力、穩定現金流與較佳
財務體質的企業，避免過度集中於高度依賴外部融資的 AI業者。
2. 美債避險功能重新受到重視，中天期風險報酬相對均衡：Fed升息預期仍使債券市場
面臨利率波動，但隨著全球股市風險偏好下降，美國公債的避險需求已明顯升高。11
月 20日美股重挫，10年期美債殖利率大幅下降，顯示部分資金轉向相對安全的固定
收益資產。儘管 Fed政策尚未明朗，且通膨仍可能造成殖利率反覆波動，但目前美國
公債殖利率仍具有一定吸引力與防禦價值。從資產類別角度來看，我們對中天期美債
維持相對正向看法，主要考量其殖利率水準及利率敏感度的風險報酬較為均衡；超長
天期公債則仍須面對財政赤字及期限溢價風險。投資級債方面，雖然市場對 AI融資風

險的疑慮升高，但整體信用品質仍相對穩健，中期展望維持偏正向，惟須留意信用利
差是否進一步擴大。
圖二:近一年費城半導體走勢

Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 黃仁勳閃電回歸輝達, 但AI投資放緩及監管壓力依舊: 黃仁勳重 擔任輝達CEO, 雖一 激勵股 大漲,
但仍需面對   會聽 會及 監會調查, 科技監管升 , 同時CSP放緩資本支出疑慮仍存。
• AI疑慮放大市場利空, 美台股市有過度恐慌現象: 301關稅一 引發股 大跌, 但實際影響有限, 股 止
穩關鍵仍是CSP資本支出放緩的不確定性。VIX維持在去年關稅戰以來高檔, 投資人有過 恐慌疑慮。
• 10月PCE符合預期, 避險需求帶動美債殖利率下滑: 10月PCE反映通膨黏性仍存, 2027年聯準會仍有升
息可能, 支持  高位震盪, 但近   殖利率因避險資金流入而走降。
2. 後續看點:
• 黃仁勳眾議院閉門訪談、參議院聽 會(12/1、12/3)、FOMC利率會議(12/10)
策略思維
1) 股市−增速放緩, 但成長格局未變:   對AI投資效益及資料 心建置疑慮升溫, 科技股大幅修正。然而, 目
前主要瓶頸來自供給, 而非需求轉弱; 預計2028年EPS增速放緩而不致轉負, 震盪放大但長多行情不致改變。

2) 債市−高利環境不變, 短線資金flight-to-cash: 升息預 、財政紀律及資金環 趨緊, 均將影響利率環
higher for longer; 惟在  波動加劇背景下, 資金流入  , 短線降低殖利率走勢。
3) 匯市−短線美元偏強震盪: 升息預 帶動    走強, Fed官員偏鷹基調亦將對  形成支撐。若未來地緣
溢價風險能延續降溫、油價走勢緩步回落, 將限縮    大幅 行空間 。

資本支出成長放緩, 仍可能引導半導體銷售放緩 資本支出下修, 企業獲利也面臨下修壓力
註: 8大CSP包括谷歌、微軟、亞馬遜、Meta、甲骨文、SpaceX、CoreWeave、Nebius。
2026年  半導體銷售為今年9月 據年化後結果。
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/11/17。
• 黃仁勳回歸, 確實有助於穩定AI產業發展方向, 持續推動資料 心建置、技術革 及半導體產能擴張, 也讓AI長
成長趨勢得以延續。不過, 相較於產業規模持續擴大, 市場更應關注資本支出的成長速度是否開始放緩, 畢竟企業
獲利動能與市場評價, 最終仍取決於新增投資能否持續轉化為營收。
• 以8大CSP業者為例, 今年資本支出年增率超過100%, 成長動能明顯見高, 但預估2027年將降至約6成, 2028年甚至
可能進一步放緩至僅1成。這意味著AI產業雖然仍處於擴張階段, 但投資成長的邊際效益可能逐步下降, 後續企業
營收與獲利成長也將面臨放緩壓力。

AI熱潮與2000年網路泡沫比較 目前估值合理, 並未異常攀高
資料來源: Bloomberg,  泰世華銀行整理。
• 近 科技股回跌, 讓  再 聯想到2000年網路泡沫, 但兩者仍存在本質差異。當年網際網路雖帶動大規模基礎建
設, 但線 商業模式尚未成熟 , 多 企業缺乏穩定營收與獲利, 投資難以轉化為實質現金流。相較之下, 現今AI已逐
步創造商業價值, 大型CSP的營業現金流可支應約八成資本支出, 遠高於當年的兩成。
• 此外, 目前  算力供給仍顯不足, GPU租金持續攀揚, 顯示  需求尚未見頂。估值方面, 2000年科技股本益比
一 高達50至60倍, 現今則約20倍 下。換言之 , 雖然AI投資已大幅投入不少資金, 但無論企業獲利、現金流或評
價水準, 均與當年存在明顯差異, 不宜直接將兩者畫上等號。

ECB｜ 洲大 的 一輪職位交換, 對  仍將構成壓力
• 11/18  推出Markus Brunnermeier角逐ECB首席經濟學家職位, 市場預估Pablo接任ECB總裁機率接近100%。

為近期歐元自身少數具有意義的人事利空。
• 此外, 如果法 判斷下一屆總統大選右派陣營勝率提高, 那麼讓提早卸任的Lagarde明年初輔選後, 轉戰 盟或 際
組織體系, 法 反而能在政治風險升高前, 先鎖定一部分 洲權力資產。 洲菁 建制派的未雨綢繆, 還有顧經濟
的選情壓力下,   的政治壓力依舊存在。

## Document 092: 2026～2027年度日本経済見通し（2026年11月）（2026年7-9月期GDP1次速報後改定）

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026～2027年度日本経済見通し（2026年11月）（2026年7-9月期GDP1次速報後改定）.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026年11月 16日

1/5 
2026～2027年度日本経済見通し（2026年 11月） 
（2026年 7-9月期 GDP1次速報後改定） 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

日本・国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）成長率予測値

2026年度予測 実質＋０．９％ （前回 ＋１．０％） 
 名目＋３．６％ （前回 ＋３．２％）

2027年度予測 実質＋１．２％ （前回 ＋１．２％） 
 名目＋３．４％ （前回 ＋３．０％）

※前回は 2026年９月８日時点の弊社予測値

○ 実質ＧＤＰ成長率の見通しは 2026年度が＋0.9％（９月時点予測：＋1.0％）、27年度が＋1.2％
（同＋1.2％）である。暦年では 26年が＋0.9％（同＋0.9％）、27年が＋1.0％（同＋1.0％）とな
る。26年７-９月期のＧＤＰ成長率が前回予測時点での想定を下回ったことを反映し、26年度の
成長率見通しを若干下方修正したが、先行きの景気見通しに大きな変更はない。26年度後半は物
価上昇率の再加速が景気の重荷となるものの、企業部門の底堅さが下支えとなり、緩やかな回復
基調は維持されると予想する。また、27年度にはコスト上昇圧力が徐々に和らぐことに加え、食
料品の消費税減税による押し上げもあり、景気の回復感が徐々に強まるとみている。

○ 今回の予測では、2027年４月から２年間、飲食料品にかかる消費税率を１％に引き下げ、あわせ
て中低所得の就業者を対象に年間 6000億円程度の就業者負担軽減支援金を支給することを前提と
している。家計の購買力増大に伴って、27年度の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は0.2％ポイント強押し上げ
られると想定している。また、27年度の消費者物価指数（総合）の前年比上昇率は▲1.4％ポイン
ト、消費者物価指数（生鮮除く総合）は▲1.2％ポイント程度押し下げられるとみられる。

○ 26年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比年率▲0.1％と、４四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となっ
た。個人消費が減少したほか、住宅投資や公共投資も成長率を押し下げた。ただし、個人消費の
減少には、４-６月期にみられた自動車やエアコンなどの耐久財需要の反動や、８～９月の天候不

### Page 2

2/5 
順といった一時的な要因が大きく影響したとみられる。一方、設備投資は高い伸びとなり、企業
部門の底堅さが確認された。前期まで３四半期連続で比較的高い成長が続いていたことも踏まえ
れば、今回の小幅なマイナス成長を悲観視する必要はない。家計と企業の回復に濃淡はみられる
ものの、日本経済は均してみれば緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

○ 26年10-12月期は前期比年率＋0.6％と、再びプラス成長に戻ると予想する。７-９月期に個人消
費を押し下げた耐久財需要の反動や天候不順といった一時的な要因が和らぐことに加え、名目賃
金の高い伸びや雇用の改善が消費を下支えするとみられる。設備投資についても、高水準の企業
収益や人手不足を背景とした省力化・デジタル関連の投資需要は根強く、増加基調を維持するだ
ろう。輸出も、海外景気の底堅さや世界的なＡＩ関連需要の拡大を背景に、緩やかな増加が期待
できる。個人消費は力強さを欠くものの、設備投資や輸出が下支えとなることで、景気の緩やか
な回復基調は維持されるだろう。

○ 26年度後半については、既往の資源高や原材料費、物流費などの上昇が時間差を伴って川下へ波
及することが、景気の下押し要因となる。食料品や日用品、耐久財など幅広い品目への価格転嫁
が進むことに加え、ガソリン補助金の段階的縮小も物価を押し上げる。こうした物価上昇率の再
加速によって、足元で続いている実質賃金の改善が一服し、家計の実質購買力が再び圧迫される
可能性がある。また、企業にとっても原材料費や物流費などの上昇が収益の重荷となるだろう。

○ もっとも、こうしたコスト上昇による下押しに対して、高水準の企業収益が一定の緩衝材となる
ことに加え、ＡＩ関連需要の拡大が半導体製造装置や電子部品などの輸出・生産、設備投資を押
し上げていることは景気の下支え要因となる。人手不足を背景とした省力化投資やデジタル関連
投資への需要も根強く、設備投資は増加基調を維持するとみられる。また、海外景気の底堅さも
輸出を支えるだろう。個人消費についても、物価高による下押し圧力が強まるものの、高い賃上
げや底堅い雇用環境が支えとなり、腰折れする可能性は低い。家計部門は力強さを欠く一方、企
業部門は堅調さを維持しており、日本経済は緩やかな回復基調を続けると予想する。

○ 27年度については、原油高や原材料費上昇による物価上昇圧力が徐々に減衰することで、景気の
回復感は次第に強まるとみている。26年秋の原油高の影響が時間差を伴って波及するため、年度
前半は物価上昇圧力がなお残るものの、年度後半にかけて価格転嫁の動きは徐々に落ち着くと予
想する。物価上昇率の鈍化によって家計の実質購買力が改善し、高い賃上げもあいまって、個人
消費の回復力が強まるだろう。食料品への消費税減税と就業者負担軽減支援金の支給も消費を押
し上げる要因となる。企業部門では、コスト上昇圧力の緩和によって収益環境が改善することも
支えとなり、省力化やデジタル・ＡＩ関連を中心とした設備投資が増加する見込みである。輸出
についても、海外景気の底堅さやＡＩ関連需要が引き続き下支え要因となる。物価面からの逆風
が和らぐことで、家計と企業の双方で内需の持ち直しが進み、27年度の景気回復の勢いは 26年度

### Page 3

3/5 
よりも強まると予想する。

○ 先行きのリスクとして注意が必要なのは、米国を中心としたＡＩ関連投資の持続性である。米中
間選挙後、州・地方政府によるデータセンター建設への規制強化が進めば、計画の延期や投資コ
ストの増加を通じて、ＡＩ関連投資の拡大ペースが鈍化する可能性がある。加えて、巨額投資の
収益性や資金調達の持続性に対する懸念も強まっている。これまでの米国景気はＡＩ投資の拡大
と株高に支えられてきた面が大きいため、仮にＡＩブームに変調が生じれば、その影響はハイテ
ク業界にとどまらず、米国経済、ひいては世界経済に波及する恐れがある。ＡＩ関連投資が大幅
に減速すれば、日本の半導体製造装置や電子部品などの輸出・生産、設備投資にも悪影響が及ぶ
だろう。もっとも、建設規制の強化とＡＩ需要そのものの減少は別問題である。ＡＩの活用拡大
に伴う計算需要は引き続き極めて強く、大手テクノロジー企業の投資意欲も旺盛である。既に着
工済みのデータセンターや契約済みの設備投資案件も多く、規制の影響を受けて投資先の地域を
変更する動きも考えられる。ＡＩ関連投資の拡大ペースは幾分鈍化する可能性があるものの、失
速するとは考えにくく、先行きも高水準の投資が続くと予想している。

○ 消費者物価指数（生鮮食品除く総合、ＣＰＩコア）は 26年度が＋2.3％（９月時点予測：＋
2.4％）、27年度が＋1.3％（同＋1.1％）と予想する。26年９月のＣＰＩコアは前年比＋2.1％と
９カ月ぶりの＋２％台となったが、年度後半にかけて一段と上昇率が高まる可能性が高い。食料
品価格は足元で鈍化傾向に歯止めがかかりつつあり、日用品でも値上げの動きが明確化してい
る。26年春の資源価格上昇や調達難の影響に加え、包装資材費や物流費などのコスト増が時間差
を伴って川下へ転嫁されることが物価を押し上げる。ＡＩ需要の拡大や円安の影響による耐久財
価格の上昇も続くとみられる。また、既往の燃料価格上昇は電気・ガス料金に波及するほか、ガ
ソリン補助金の段階的縮小も押し上げ要因となる。なお、冬場の電気・ガス料金補助は一定の押
し下げ要因となるが、前年比への影響は限定的とみられる。企業の価格転嫁姿勢が積極化してい
ることもあり、27年初にかけてＣＰＩコアは前年比＋３％を上回る可能性が高い。

○ 27年度については、26年秋の原油高の影響が時間差を伴って川下へ波及するため、年度前半は物
価上昇圧力が根強く残るとみられる。もっとも、原油高や原材料費上昇によるコストプッシュ圧
力は次第に弱まり、消費税要因を除いても、年度後半にかけて物価上昇率は緩やかに鈍化してい
くと予想する。食料品や日用品、耐久財などへの価格転嫁の動きも次第に落ち着き、物価面から
の家計への下押し圧力が和らぐことで、個人消費の持ち直しが後押しされるだろう。一方、高い
賃上げに伴う人件費上昇や人手不足、企業の積極的な価格設定姿勢は続くとみられ、サービス価
格を中心とした基調的な物価上昇圧力は維持されるとみている。なお、今回の予測では、27年４
月から食料品の消費税率を８％から１％に引き下げることを前提としており、これにより 27年度
のＣＰＩコア上昇率が約1.2％ポイント押し下げられると想定している。

### Page 4

4/5 
○ 金融政策について、日銀は 26年12月の金融政策決定会合で政策金利を1.25％から1.50％に引き
上げると予想する。日銀は 10月会合で、基調的な物価上昇率が２％目標と概ね整合的な水準に達
したとの判断を示し、金融政策運営の重点は「２％への到達」から「２％での定着」へと移っ
た。今後は基調的な物価上昇率を２％程度で安定させることが重要になるが、物価上振れリスク
が残るなか、現在の金融環境はなお緩和的であり、日銀は中立金利に向けて金融緩和の度合いを
縮小していくと考えられる。

○ その後も27年３月に1.75％、６月に2.00％へと、３カ月程度の間隔で追加利上げを行うと予想
する。27年度にはコスト要因による物価上昇圧力が徐々に弱まるとみられるものの、賃金上昇や
人手不足を背景とした基調的な物価上昇圧力は維持されるだろう。物価上振れへの警戒が残るな
か、日銀は段階的な利上げを継続すると予想する。なお、今回の利上げ局面の最終到達点（ター
ミナルレート）は2.0％と見込むが、中立金利の水準には大きな不確実性がある。基調的な物価上
昇率が想定以上に上振れれば 2.0％を超える利上げもあり得るだろう。一方、景気の下振れや既往
の利上げの累積的な影響が想定以上に強まる場合には、利上げペースが鈍化する可能性もある。

黃璽銘 hsiming.huang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
規模效益顯現

永豐觀點 
AI 伺服器為成長主軸，規模效益顯現，觀察政策長期對 AI 資料中心建置的影響。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海 3Q26 財報、4Q26 展望優於預期。2027 年維持
以 AI 伺服器為成長主軸，公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求仍大於供給，關鍵
零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業 AI 資料中心建置
時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效
益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。研究部預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後
EPS 19.33 元/25.51 元。維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車、數位醫療、機器人、AI、半導體、5G 等領域。主要客戶為 Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。3Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 54%、電腦終端 12%、元件及其他 5%。 
3Q26 財報優於預期：鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，營益率 3.83%，優於預估的
5.93%、3.77%。鴻海營收規模放大，營運槓桿效益顯現。業外收入貢獻 33 億元，
稅後淨利 775 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元，優於預估 5.10 元。3Q26 鴻海存貨金額
1.5 兆，季增 10%，年增 27%，存貨天數 45.7 天，季減 3 天，年減 4 天。存貨狀
況尚屬健康。 
4Q26 展望略優於預期：鴻海展望 4Q26 營運，AI 伺服器出貨量季增高雙位數(部
分專案採 Consignment 商業模式)，預期 ASIC 及 GPU 出貨續增，Vera Rubin AI
伺服器機櫃單價跳升，推估雲端網路營收季增約 15%。消費性產品 iPhone 維持
強勁出貨動能，PC 需求則持續疲弱季減。研究部略為上修 4Q26 營收預估季增
17%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.99 元。 
AI 伺服器為鴻海營運增長動能 留意長期資料中心建置影響性：因應客戶 AI 建置
需求，鴻海持續支出投入產能，主要客戶為美系大型 CSP、AI 模型商及 Neocloud，
降低資金及庫存風險。並透過與客戶改成採用 Consignment 商業模式，提高營運
資金效率，並優化獲利率表現。研究部預期鴻海維持 Vera Rubin 的主要供應商角
色，整體市占率維持達 40%以上，同時鴻海 2027 年將增加美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺
服器機櫃供應，為 2027 年營運增長動能。公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求
仍大於供給，關鍵零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業
AI 資料中心建置時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同
時體現獲利率效益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。 
綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 11.2 兆，年增 38%，毛利率 6.0%，營益率
3.7%，稅後淨利 2,710 億，年增 43%，稅後 EPS 19.33 元。2027 年營收 15.5 兆
元，年增 38%，毛利率 5.4%，營益率 3.4%，稅後淨利 3,562 億元，年增 31%，
稅後 EPS 25.51 元。

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
WT Microelectronics Co. Ltd.

(3036.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$296.00 NT$174.00
WPG Holdings (3702.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$160.00 NT$79.00
Despite strong 3Q26 results and resilient near-term guidance at
both WT Microelectronics and WPG Holdings, we see visibility
risks into 2028. Memory price normalization and rising USD
funding costs prompt us to trim expectations across Greater
China semi distributors.
Peaking momentum meets rising 2027-28 headwinds: Both WT and WPG posted

record top-lines and profit scale in 3Q26 on AI server buildouts and sharp memory
price inflation; yet, we are wary of several late-cycle pressures. First, on AI
infrastructure, our US team recently shifted ~5 GW of US data center capacity from
2028 into 2029 post-US midterms (link), introducing procurement timing risk.
Second, we caution that memory may still be cyclical after all, and we may now be
late into the cycle; (Old Memory: Peak Pricing Power?; Memory – Key Debate Post

Mid-terms (Shawn Kim)).  Third, elevated memory prices are already weighing on
traditional consumer end markets. Finally, rapid revenue expansion has driven heavy
working capital outflows and debt accumulation just as our US Economics team
forecasts two 25 bps Fed hikes in Dec and Mar to 4.25%-4.50%, keeping USD
financing costs elevated (link).
WT Microelectronics (3036.TW) 3Q26 review (link): For 4Q26, management
guided revenue to reaccelerate to a midpoint of NT$750bn (+26% Q/Q, +119% Y/Y)

on high double-digit Q/Q data center growth, with GM stepping down to a midpoint
of 3.24% (-0.36ppt Q/Q) on mix. Net profit guidance was a midpoint of NT$11.5bn
(+12% Q/Q, +17% Y/Y). 4Q26 financing costs were guided up to NT$2.7bn (vs. NT
$1.94bn in 3Q26) with no timeline for operating cash flow turning positive.
WPG Holdings (3702.TW) 3Q26 review: 3Q26 revenue was NT$510bn (+11.8% Q/Q,
+108.6% Y/Y), GM was 4.42%, and net profit was NT$10.39bn (+20.5% Q/Q,

+226.9% Y/Y). Memory surged to 58% of 3Q26 revenue (from ~25% historically)
and compute (AI and server) reached 52%, while communication/mobile mix was 11%
and consumer mix was 9%. Management noted its ship-and-debit model mitigates
inventory write-down risk, and its LaaS platform grew GMV 75.6% Y/Y (revenue
+15.5% Y/Y). For 4Q26, WPG guided revenue at a midpoint of NT$505bn, -1.0% Q/Q,
GM at a midpoint of 4.40%, and EPS at a midpoint of NT$5.47. Financial cost/sales

as at ~0.8% (two-thirds USD-denominated).
Stock implications: WT: downgrading to EW, PT cut to NT$174. 2026/27/28 EPS
revised by +1%/-6%/-5%. WPG: downgrading to EW, PT cut to NT$79. 2026/27/28
EPS revised by +46%/+10%/-25%.

WT Micro: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$174: We update our residual income model to reflect
earnings estimate revisions. We reduce our terminal growth rate assumption from 3%  to
2%, in line with longer term global macro economic growth rate. We cut our intermediate
growth rate assumption from 8.0% to 4.0% to reflect demand uncertainties in the end
market. We also raise our beta estimate from 0.90 to 1.10 to reflect potentially peaking

growth and demand uncertainties, and this leads to an increased cost of equity from 13.3%
to 15.6%. Other key RI assumptions are unchanged: 2.0% risk-free rate, and  cash dividend
payout ratio of 85%. We believe WT deserves a high beta due to the high liabilities to
equity ratio (5.92x as of 3Q26) and the strong correlation with the wider Taiwan stock
market due to the nature of the distribution business.
We downgrade WT to EW. We believe the risk factors we’re seeing are likely to persist

and the stock price will likely remain under pressure unless there is further clarity or
policy reversals; we do not recommend accumulation even though the stock price has
corrected significantly from its peak in May.

WPG: Estimate Revisions Summary
We revise 2026-28e EPS by +46%/+10%/-25% each:   This factors in 2-3Q26 actual results
and 4Q26 guidance, which led to stronger 2026 estimates, as well as some near-term
resilience into 2027 with sustained memory shipment volume. Further into 2027 and
2028, we factor in an increasingly uncertain investment environment and therefore reduce
our estimates for non-operating income, mainly investment income.

WPG: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$79: We update our residual income model to reflect
earnings estimate revisions. We also raise our beta estimate from 1.3 to 1.5 to reflect
potentially peaking growth and demand uncertainties, and raised our risk premium from
6% to 8% to reflect market volatilities; this leads to an increased cost of equity from 9.5%
to 13.9%. We also raise our risk free rate from 1.5% to 2.0% to reflect rising funding costs.

Our intermediate growth rate is reduced from 5.5% to 4.0% to reflect demand
uncertainties. Other key RI assumptions are unchanged: cash dividend payout ratio of
80%, and long-term growth rate of 2.0%. We believe WPG deserves a high beta due to its
high liabilities to equity ratio (5.85x as of 3Q26) and the strong correlation to the wider
Taiwan stock market due to the nature of the distribution business.
We downgrade WPG to EW. We believe the risk factors we’re seeing are likely to persist

and the stock price will likely remain under pressure unless there is further clarity or
policy reversals; we do not recommend accumulation even though the stock price has
corrected significantly from its peak in August.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
What’s New from Taiwan Corporate Day: Online UPS Leads 2027E 
Growth; HVDC Moves Toward Qualification 
CITI'S TAKE 
Management expects online UPS to lead in 2027E, growing 10-15% y/y on 
sustained outsourcing and rising demand for high-power UPS, with offline 
up 5-10% y/y. Three tier 1 western vendor projects underpin Voltronic’s 
HVDC pipeline. The lead program targets qualification shipments in 1Q27E 
and mass production in 1H28E. Mgmt sees HVDC contributing 10% of group 
sales in 2028E. We lower our TP to NT$1,000 (26x 27E EPS) reflecting 
mostly lowered earnings due to higher input costs and ongoing inverter 
competition. Stay Buy in view of sustained UPS outsourcing, rising high-
power demand and advancing HVDC projects. 
HVDC progress: added one new project — M
gmt. noted currently there are three project under development and the progress 
remains on track. First project – Voltronic plans to ship PSUs in 1Q27E for customer 
qualification, start safety certification in 4Q27E and begin mass production in 
1H28E. Second project – the customer has requested a 499V, 1MW rack solution 
covering the full rack, including power distribution units, PSUs and BBUs. Voltronic 
expects to deliver a prototype in 1H27E. Third project – the customer recently re-
engaged Voltronic to design HVDC products after its initial interaction in Feb 2026. 
In addition to the Tier 1 UPS vendors, the mgmt. has also received inquiries from Tier 
2 UPS brands. The preliminary forecast for HVDC contribution is reaching 10% of 
group sales in 2028E. The company expects its existing facility in Taiwan to cover 
initial requirements and will assess expansion once customer volume forecasts firm 
up. 
UPS outlook: outsourcing and high-power demand support the growth — Online 
UPS sales rose 22% y/y in 3Q26 and mgmt. expects it to remain the growth driver in 
2027E with sales up 10-15% y/y. The ongoing outsourcing trend from the Tier 1 UPS 
vendors and rising demand for high-power online-UPS are the key drivers. High 
power UPS made up 7% of group sales in 3Q and carried a 31% GPM. Mgmt. expects 
high-power UPS to reach c.10% of group sales in 2027E. Offline UPS sales grew 27% 
y/y in 3Q26 (helped by a low base), and mgmt. expects it to grow 5-10% y/y in 2027E.

### Page 3

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
24 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Inverter: portfolio shift underway yet margins likely remain under pressure —
Mgmt expects inverter sales to be flat y/y in 2027E after a 35% y/y dip in 3Q26,
impacted mostly by the Middle East conflict. Voltronic began to phase out low-
margin products in 3Q26 (off-grid) and is prioritizing higher-power, commercial-
grade products where customers require more customization. It is also expanding in

NA and India. India now accounts for 20% of inverter sales, supported by residential
subsidies. Intense price competition from Chinese suppliers, limited ability to pass
through higher input costs and the unfavorable geo mix are likely to keep margins
under pressure during the transition in our view.
Implications — We trim our 2026-2028E EPS estimates 9-15% mainly to reflect
lower GPM assumptions in view of elevated input costs and continued inverter

pricing and mix pressure. We trim our TP to NT$,1000 from NT$1,050 to reflect lower
earnings which is based on a 26x 2027E, on par with its historical average. We stay
Buy in view of continued outsourcing by western UPS vendors which supports share
gains and a richer mix of online and higher-power UPS supporting growth and
profitability. Beyond UPS, Voltronic’s relationships with Tier 1 power vendors and
experience in high-voltage systems position it to capture more content value as

HVDC programs move toward commercialization.

### Page 5

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
24 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Voltronic Power
Company description
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014,
Voltronic is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model, which avoids
direct competition with customers and has helped the company establish

long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS brands. The company is
headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing facilities in Taiwan,
China and Vietnam.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Voltronic, supported by an earnings recovery,
structural UPS outsourcing and emerging opportunities in AIDC power
infrastructure. Its customization capabilities and longstanding relationships
with western power vendors position it to capture additional outsourcing

orders and expand into higher power products. HVDC power racks offer a L-T
growth opportunity and potential re-rating catalyst. We view 2026E as
earnings trough, with a richer online UPS mix and operating leverage driving a
28% earnings rebound in 2027E.
Valuation
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 26x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1000, broadly in line with its long term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s

established earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating
leverage to its profitability.
Risks
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include: 1) Slower UPS
demand or outsourcing gain. 2) Less favorable product mix and margin
pressure. 3) Persistent PV inverter competition. 4) Weaker macro economy

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

higher UTR and internal efficiency gains alongside pricing. UTR exceeded 
90% across standard and specialty lines. MLCC margins now rank above 
inductors and sit close to the corp. average, a clear sign of mix upgrade. For 
4Q26, mgmt. guided slight growth in revenue, GPM and OPM, with the BB 
ratio near 2 and Oct-26 revenue still up 3% MoM in a holiday-heavy month.
• 2027 expansion targets the tightest lines - Yageo has approved a 10-15% 
group capacity expansion for 2027, with equipment already ordered. The 
additions re concentrated in MLCC and tantalum, currently the two tightest 
product lines, and further projects remain under review. Mgmt. expects the 
supply gap to persist in 2027, with market growth supported by data center 
projects already approved and under construction, although visibility into 
2028 could be slightly lower. AI now accounts for 20% of total revenue, 
with tantalum, resistor, MLCC having the highest exposure. Yageo has kept 
serving standard orders throughout the cycle, since customers who switch 
suppliers rarely return once requalification is complete.
• Pricing discipline and flexible LTAs - The Jul-26 price adjustment is 
flowing through to contract customers over 1-2 quarters, with a larger 
contribution in 4Q26 and a full run-rate from 1Q27. Mgmt. will not rule out 
further adjustments if the market stays tight. LTAs carry a pricing 
framework that floats with the market, with no ceiling and no customer 
prepayment, and they are capped at 40% of capacity to preserve flexibility. 
Channel inventory remains healthy. Distributors in the US and EU are still 
restocking industrial and aerospace inventory from below safety levels,

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,000, based on 23x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 23x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022 but in line with global peers average. During this period the company has
gone through the cycles, successfully integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver

stable margins and earnings.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 223: [000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

목표주가 (하향) 155,000 원 
현재주가 95,600 원 
상승여력 62.1 %

## Document 231: [005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up현대차(005380) 3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up3Q26 Review_ 4분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up - 기업.pdf

이재일 CFA 
6183 / lee.jae-il@eugenefn.com

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500 원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 244: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다 - 기업

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 254: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

표5  타법인 주식 양수결정: PolyPeptide Group AG 인수 개요 
항목 내용 
대상회사 PolyPeptide Group AG (스위스 펩타이드 CDMO 기업) 
양수금액 2,706,163,546,103원 (주당 매수가 CHF 44.31)

## Document 275: AI交易回暖，纳指100期货盘前涨2.5%，科技股集体大涨，闪迪暴涨10%，金银大涨，美债价格回落

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易回暖，纳指100期货盘前涨2.5%，科技股集体大涨，闪迪暴涨10%，金银大涨，美债价格回落.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美国股指期货全线⾛⾼，感恩节假期后华尔街的反弹⾏情有望延续，标普500指数期货上涨
1.7%。10年期美债收益率升⾄4.92%，美元指数下跌0.1%。⻩⾦价格上涨2.6%，现货⽩
银涨超6%，布伦特原油涨1%。
摘要：
⼈⼯智能交易回暖提振市场⻛险偏好，全球股市普涨。
周五，美国股指期货全线⾛⾼，预⽰感恩节假期后华尔街的反弹⾏情有望延续。纳斯达克100指
数期货上涨2.5%，标普500指数期货上涨1.7%；MSCI亚太指数上涨1.8%，延续周三美股反弹
势头。
市场⻛险偏好回升，投资者减持避险资产，美国国债价格随之下跌。感恩节假期结束后，美债
现货交易于周五恢复，10年期美债收益率上升6个基点⾄4.92%。美元⼩幅⾛低，美元指数下跌
0.1%，但仍有望创下两年来最⻓的⽉度连涨纪录。
商品市场⽅⾯，⻩⾦价格上涨2.6%，接近每盎司3660美元，现货⽩银涨超6%；布伦特原油涨
1%⾄92.36美元/桶。
Reed Capital Partners⾸席投资官Gerald Gan表⽰：“市场似乎正在企稳，尽管这可能只是短暂

的反弹，但我们的基本预期仍然认为，到年底前市场将迎来⼀波最终的上涨⾏情。”
核⼼市场⾛势如下：
标普500指数期货上涨近2%；纳斯达克100指数期货上涨2.6%。
美股盘前，英伟达涨超4%，Anthropic涨超6%，SpaceX涨超4%；纳指期货盘前时段涨约
2.8%，闪迪美股盘前涨超10%。美光科技盘前涨超6%，SK海⼒⼠涨超6%，特斯拉涨超
2%。
欧股开盘多数上涨，欧洲斯托克50指数涨0.84%，英国富时100指数涨0.35%，法国CAC40
指数持平，德国DAX指数涨0.47%。
⽇经225指数收盘上涨2.1%，报58,711.11点。⽇本东证指数收盘上涨1.7%，报3,815.20点。
韩国⾸尔综指收涨4.34%，报4915.68点。
10年期美国国债收益率上涨6个基点⾄4.92%。
印度10年期国债收益率上⾏5个基点⾄7.28%。
彭博美元现货指数下跌0.1%。
印度卢⽐兑美元下跌0.5%，⾄92.8625。
现货⻩⾦⽇内涨幅达2.6%，报3660美元/盎司。
布伦特原油涨1%⾄92.36美元/桶。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

抛售潮暂缓，美股期指⾛⾼，AI交易能否持续反弹仍待观察
⼈⼯智能交易能否延续反弹，仍是市场关注的焦点。尽管科技股带动全球股市回暖，但投资者
对AI巨额资本投⼊能否转化为⾜够回报仍存疑虑。经历近期市场剧烈波动后，交易员对进⼀步加
仓保持谨慎，市场复苏的持续性仍有待检验。
科技股成为亚太市场反弹的主要动⼒，也是MSCI亚太指数中表现最强的板块。韩国芯⽚巨头SK
海⼒⼠、三星电⼦以及⽇本铠侠控股股价均有所上涨，显⽰AI产业链仍是资⾦关注的核⼼。
Astris Advisory Japan战略主管Neil Newman表⽰，当前AI和半导体交易似乎仍处于停滞状态，
市场尚未完全摆脱⻛险。他指出，近期市场动荡加剧了投资者的谨慎情绪，AI相关资产能否真正
企稳仍不明朗。
⻛险偏好回升削弱避险需求，美债收益率全线上⾏
股市情绪回暖削弱了投资者对避险资产的需求，美国国债价格随之下跌，收益率曲线各期限美
债收益率均⾄少上升3个基点。美国感恩节假期结束后，美债现货交易于周五恢复。
道明证券新加坡策略师Prashant Newnaha表⽰，⻛险偏好回升是推动美债收益率上⾏的主要因

素。周三美国科技股（周四美股因感恩节休市）尾盘的反弹势头延续⾄亚洲交易时段，进⼀步
提振了市场情绪。
中东供应担忧⽀撑油价，美元⾛软提振⻩⾦
受中东能源设施及油轮遇袭影响，市场对原油出⼝供应的担忧升温，油价有望迎来本⽉⾸次周
线上涨。布伦特原油涨1%⾄92.36美元每桶。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
股市反弹、美元⾛软抵消了美联储加息预期的压⼒，现货⻩⾦⽇内上涨2.6%，报每盎司3660美
元。
现货⽩银涨超6%。

## Document 311: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-13).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years.1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.

6 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What is our view of the macro and market landscape heading
into 2027 and beyond?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
A supply side-led economic expansion continues, driven by capital investment in the AI
buildout, solid consumer spending and fiscal tailwinds still blowing, albeit with less force.
This creates a backdrop that keeps nominal growth well-above trend and, importantly,

above the interest cost on national debt.
The interest rate backdrop largely remains the same as of today, but, instead of the
sharp increases that we experienced in 2026, the curve should settle down, and yields
should remain relatively range bound. The Fed is likely to tap on the breaks a couple of
more times before reassessing new inflation, growth and employment trends later in
2027.
Concerns surrounding credit risk and "circular" financing are likely to rise the further we

move into the AI buildout, but we do not expect a default cycle to develop. Rather, we
expect the sharp backup in yields to place pressure namely on lower-quality credits in
areas having a difficult time building the cash flow necessary to pay the higher interest
costs.
We expect a broadening out within the market but technology to still maintain its
heavyweight positioning. As we move from training and evolving the large language
models to agentic, and physical AI new leaders are likely to develop. In addition, we

anticipate AI adopters such as biotechnology, transport, logistics, advance manufacturing
and financial companies to enter new growth and productivity phases.
Lastly, overall earnings growth in the U.S. should be low to mid double-digit percent
stages in 2027, which is about half of the growth from 2026 thus far but still around
double from normal levels.
Credit risks developing and higher energy prices for longer are the main risks to our bull
case for Equities extending further into 2027 and beyond.

Index Definitions
ecurities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

University of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index is a monthly survey of consumer confidence levels in the United States conducted by the University of Michigan.
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence measures what consumers are feeling about their expected financial situation, whether that's optimistic or pessimistic.
Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and

services.
Nasdaq Composite Index is a stock market measure that tracks the performance of more than 3,000 stocks listed on the Nasdaq exchange.
MSCI Emerging Markets c is a stock market index that tracks the performance of large-cap and mid-cap companies across 24 developing and fast-growing nations.
MSCI All-Country World Index ex USA is a market-capitalization-weighted stock market index that tracks large- and mid-cap equity performance across developed and emerging markets

worldwide, excluding the United States.
MSCI Europe, Australasia, and the Far East (EAFE) Index is a stock market index that measures the performance of large- and mid-cap companies across 21 developed.
Dow Jones Industrial Average Index is a stock market index that tracks 30 major public companies listed on the New York Stock Exchange (NYSE) and Nasdaq.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is an alternative version of the standard S&P 500 that gives every constituent company an identical weight of 0.2% during each quarterly rebalance, rather

than weighting companies by their market capitalization.
Russell 2000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of approximately 2,000 small-cap (small-capitalization) public companies in the United States.
S&P 500 sub-sectors and industry groups Global Industry Classification Standard (GICS®)/S&P 500 Total Return Index, including Information Technology Total Return (TR) USD;
Consumer Discretionary TR USD; Industrials TR USD; Real Estate TR USD; Communication Services TR USD; Materials  TR USD; Financials TR USD; Consumer Staples TR USD; Utilities TR USD;

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.
Nonfinancial assets, such as closely held businesses, real estate, fine art, oil, gas and mineral properties, and timber, farm and ranch land, are complex in nature and involve risks including total

## Document 324: Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: risks mount
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:31 November 20 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s labour market disappointed again in October, employment falling 43.5k following
September’s 36.4k decline. On a three-month average basis, job gains over the year have now slowed
to 1.1%yr, materially below current growth in the working-age population, 1.7%yr. As a result, the

unemployment rate rose to a new post-pandemic high of 4.9% despite the participation rate falling
another 0.2ppts to 66.6%.

### Page 2

While employment growth’s descent initially reflected softer momentum in hiring, now we are seeing
evidence of a material increase in redundancies. Most affected workers look to have remained in
employment to date, but persistent job shedding raises the risk of a faster and more persistent uptrend
in the unemployment rate, potentially above our baseline peak forecast of 5.2% (at mid-2027) and most
certainly before the late-2028 peak of 5.1% the RBA expects.

As such, the robust 1.1% (3.5%yr) rise in the Q3 Wage Price Index warrants close scrutiny. The Q3
increase was skewed towards those on awards and enterprise arrangements – pay outcomes directly
tied to the Fair Work Commission’s decision and collective bargaining. Individual arrangements
meanwhile, the most responsive to current and expected labour market conditions, accounted for their
smallest share of a Q3 wage increase since 2019. Business survey detail also suggests labour cost

growth will normalise quickly after the award increase.
Deteriorating economic conditions are also increasingly evident in housing. The Westpac Housing
Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in November, among the weakest readings since 2005. Interestingly,
there remains considerable latent demand: almost 13% of consumers intend to purchase a home over
the next year, and relatively few plan to sell. For now, though, weak sentiment, high interest rates and

expectations of further price declines are keeping would-be buyers on the sidelines. These factors also
pose risks to the broader growth story.
In the US, abstracting from the Anthropic IPO, the outlook for inflation remained the critical theme. The
October FOMC minutes highlighted that inflation is currently being supported by tariffs and energy
prices as well as the AI buildout, with "several [members] judg[ing] that price pressures were
increasingly extending beyond industries directly affected by data center construction”. Participants

"assessed that medium- and longer-term inflation expectations continued to be consistent with the
Committee's 2 percent objective", although persistent above-target inflation is a threat to the status
quo, and perceived risks are skewed to the upside. FOMC members who spoke throughout the week
also kept the focus on price risks, and called for the FOMC to act swiftly. Governor Waller again
proposed a 50bp hike in December; others seemingly favour a 25bp hike at the December and January

meetings, as Westpac forecasts, and continuing to tighten further in 2027 if necessary.
Very clearly, the labour market and activity growth are not of concern for US policy makers. Retail sales
gave further justification for this view this week, the control group surprising to the upside with a 0.6%
gain in October after a material upward revision to September (0.5%). That said, housing is under
considerable pressure, pending home sales falling 17.1% over the past year, and housing starts back

at 2019 levels despite a 9.9% rebound in October. Persistent weakness in this sector will, in time, have
implications for both consumption and investment.
China’s October data round was the other key international development of the week. Again, it provided
a call for pro-active stimulus in scale. In the month, annual retail sales growth almost stalled at 0.4%yr
and the decline in fixed asset investment broadened outside property, with capital expenditure now

down 8.1% across the economy year-to-date. Industrial production again highlighted that its output to
satiate global demand which is holding up aggregate growth; though, even here, momentum softened
in October, annual growth in production slowing from 5.5%yr to 4.8%yr. It is not only activity that

### Page 3

Related articles
remains under pressure, but also wealth, with new / existing home prices 0.15% / 0.24% lower in the
month. As we continue to emphasise, to prove effective, stimulus needs to affect change in both activity
and wealth and do so in a sustainable manner.

## Document 345: European Economics Analyst_ Euro Area 2027 Outlook_ Resilience Put to the Test

Source PDF: all report pdf/European Economics Analyst_ Euro Area 2027 Outlook_ Resilience Put to the Test.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

After resilient growth this year, several headwinds will likely weigh on the n
economy in 2027. We expect high energy prices to slow consumption over the 
winter, tighter credit conditions to curb demand and France to stagnate until the 
elections. But global manufacturing (supported by AI capex), German fiscal 
expansion and continued structural expansion in Southern Europe should 
cushion these headwinds. We expect Euro area growth of 0.9% in 2027, below 
the recent run rate, estimated potential and consensus.  
High energy prices will likely keep headline inflation near 4% through the winter, n
while indirect effects push core inflation to a 2.9% peak in Q2. But we expect a 
short-lived hump given limited wage second-round effects, with sequential 
inflation pressures largely normalising in 2027H2. 
Above-target inflation and recent ECB commentary point to one more rate hike n
in December. A March move is possible with higher energy prices and stronger 
data, but the hurdle to faster hikes is high. We expect normalisation cuts from 
2027Q4 as the inflation shock fades, leaving our rate path below market pricing. 
Intensifying French budget tensions are the main risk. Polls suggest that Marine n
Le Pen is most likely to become President. If she is elected and wins a majority in 
Parliament, investors could question her commitment to reduce the deficit. If the 
elections fail to deliver a parliamentary majority, the prospects of continued high 
deficits could further weigh on domestic assets. French fiscal tensions could spill 
over into other high-debt countries, but the risk of area-wide contagion looks 
notably lower than in 2011-15. 
Europe also faces structural headwinds from lost competitiveness, rising n
competition from China and demographic changes. Growth industries (such as 
defence), widespread AI adoption and further internal market integration) offer 
significant opportunities, although the upcoming elections will likely hinder 
major reform progress in the near term.

### Page 2

Euro Area 2027 Outlook: Resilience Put to the Test

Following surprisingly resilient Euro area growth this year, we expect 2027 to be more 
challenging. We forecast area-wide growth of 0.9% next year, slightly below the recent 
run rate, our estimate of potential and consensus (Exhibit 1). But we expect growth to 
improve in H2 as headwinds fade and see significant variation across the Euro area, with 
firm growth in Germany and Spain, subdued momentum in Italy and stagnation in 
France.

Rising Headwinds 
We expect several headwinds to slow growth next year, especially in H1. 
First, high energy prices are likely to weigh on consumption over the winter (Exhibit 2). 
Our commodity strategists expect Brent oil and European TTF gas prices to remain 
elevated given continued disruption of Middle East supplies, with risks towards renewed 
escalation and higher prices. As a result, high headline inflation is likely to weigh 
materially on real disposable income over the winter and slow consumer spending. 
Consistent with this, consumer confidence has dropped since the summer and retail 
sales have weakened, especially in Germany.

Exhibit 1: : We Expect Trend-Like Growth in 2027

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg
13 November 2026   2
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 3

Second, tighter credit conditions look set to slow growth (Exhibit 3). Following recent 
easing, our area-wide financial condition index (GSFCI) is now only modestly tighter 
since the summer, as a weaker trade-weighted Euro has mitigated the effects of higher 
long-term yields and wider credit spreads. But survey-based measures of bank lending 
have deteriorated notably, especially in France. As a result, credit conditions are likely to 
act as a moderate headwind to growth next year, particularly on rate-sensitive domestic 
activity (such as housing and business investment).

Third, we expect the French economy to stagnate until the elections (Exhibit 4). In 
addition to tightening financial conditions, heightened political and policy uncertainty 
are likely to lead households and firms to postpone major consumption and investment 
decisions. Fiscal policy is likely to remain a contractionary force, because a large 
increase in interest payments will likely mask a modest consolidation in the primary 
balance. We forecast annual average growth of 0.1% in 2027, well below other

Exhibit 3: Tighter Credit Conditions Will Weigh on Growth

Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

forecasters and the weakest forecast among the developed economies we cover.

Sources of Resilience 
That said, we see several sources of resilience, which should buffer these headwinds and 
help growth recover in H2. 
First, we expect a continued boost from the global manufacturing cycle (Exhibit 5). The 
international industrial upswing continues, boosting factory orders and exports, 
especially in Germany. AI capex is an important driver behind this pickup, likely lifting 
Euro area growth by 0.1pp this year and next. Given spare capacity in European 
manufacturing—particularly in Germany—we see a continued cyclical export tailwind 
ahead.

Second, fiscal policy will continue to support growth in Germany (Exhibit 6). We remain 
constructive on the German fiscal rollout driven by higher defence spending and

Exhibit 4: We Expect Stagnation in France as Fiscal Uncertainty Builds

improved execution of infrastructure investment. We expect the German deficit to rise 
to 4.6% next year (from 4% in 2026) and boost German growth by 0.6pp (vs 0.7pp this 
year). That said, fiscal policy looks set to turn contractionary in France and Spain as 
national consolidation pressures build and the Recovery Fund expires. As a result, we see 
a neutral area-wide fiscal stance in 2027 after a small net boost this year, before turning 
contractionary in 2028.

Third, we see continued structural expansion in Southern Europe (Exhibit 7). Spain, in 
particular, has continued to outgrow the Euro area materially, driven by strong gains in 
employment (underpinned by immigration), higher-value-added services (including 
finance and ICT) and investment. While momentum is likely to moderate next year, we 
see continued outperformance in the South.
